- **2026-09-08 — CLAUDE — IN PROGRESS — Time-box the Deno cron tick so it stops overrunning its 45s deadline (board `c630ceed`, branch `claude/tick-deadline-1b`, Sentry `CONGRESS-TRADE-1B`).**  The tick already aborts between lanes, but the two latency lanes ignore the abort signal and hand the raw global `fetch` to the peer market-data client, so a serial run of per-ticker intraday calls keeps going past both the soft and the hard deadline.  Fix: a shared deadline-aware fetch that gives every upstream call inside the tick its own timeout, plus abort checks between snapshot groups so unfinished rows carry to the next tick.  The 45,000 ms constant is not raised — it is already overridable via `CT_TICK_DEADLINE_MS`.  Claimed Mon, Sep 8, 2026.

Web rewrites a stored `localStorage['ui-theme'] === 'sepia'` to `'light'` in place at both the pre-paint boot script and `readThemePref()`, before first paint.  iOS funnels every read of `UserDefaults['app_color_scheme']` through a new `AppAppearance.migratedPref(_:)` that does the same rewrite; `@AppStorage` picks it up automatically. - **2026-08-20 — CLAUDE — IN PR #2082 — Premium activation alerts + Codex review round (branch `claude/premium-signup-alerts`).**  Pushover on first Premium activation from either billing path, once per subscription.  Migration 0093 (renumbered twice by collisions with `admin_allowlist` and `apple_subscriptions_nullable_user`).  EIGHT Codex findings resolved, several real correctness bugs not style: **P1** `sendPushover` fetch had NO abort signal — the hazard is a request that never SETTLES, and it is awaited from the Stripe webhook and Apple redeem, so a stalled socket delays the webhook indefinitely and Stripe retries an event it thinks timed out; now bounded at 5s in the shared helper so every caller benefits.  **P1** missing rollout doc — written.  **P2** the claim was consumed BEFORE delivery, so any later failure lost the alert forever at `!isNew` rather than failing soft; claim is now RELEASED on failure, which also fixes the separate migration-window finding (auto-deploy ships code before schema, so the claim throws on first deploy).  **P2** `customer.subscription.updated` was excluded, so every card-confirmation subscription (opens `incomplete`, confirms via `updated`) became Premium with no alert; now admitted, safe because the ledger claim is the real idempotency guard.  **P2** `plan` defaulted null→monthly, announcing Premium for a user the entitlement resolver does not grant and the totals query excludes; a recognised plan is now required.  **P2** deprecated `POST /billing/apple/confirm` still grants Premium for old iOS clients but never notified; now wired with the same activationKey shape.  **P2** Apple `isNew` came from a pre-read, so two accounts redeeming the same unseen originalTransactionId could both claim newness and the LOSER could send an alert carrying its own email for the winner subscription; newness now re-checked against the persisted owner after the upsert.  Gates: `deno check` clean; 3555/3556 tests (the one failure is a known timing flake in `delivery/__tests__/sse.test.ts` — untouched by this diff, passes 5/5 in isolation); lint 404 = main baseline, zero added.  MUST run `POST /api/admin/migrate` after merge.  Receipt: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-20-premium-activation-alerts.md`.

- **2026-08-20 — CLAUDE — IN PR — Latency price snapshots: record-then-backfill, FMP removed (branch `claude/latency-snapshot-repair`).**  Pipeline recorded **7 prices out of 2955** (2937 `missed_window`, 11 `fmp_quote_http_402`).  Two root causes: (A) rows scheduled RETROSPECTIVELY from matched candidates so `due_at` was always past and the 3-min staleness guard correctly refused — cron was fine, the scheduling MODEL was wrong; (B) sole price source was FMP, now banned fleet-wide for market data (still valid as a latency competitor being probed).  Fix collapses schedule/capture into one per-row per-tick decision: within `SNAPSHOT_STALE_MS` ask ST for a live quote, else backfill from ST `/api/market/intraday` nearest-bar-at-or-after.  A delayed tick reclassifies as backfill instead of dying, so `missed_window` cannot recur.  `ct_publish` now scheduled inline at candidate mint (the one moment its anchor is genuinely "now").  +15m rung added per owner.  CRITICAL REVIEW CATCH: a single empty `200 {bars:[]}` must NOT terminate a row — until ST #2959 lands, ST collapses every intraday failure into that shape, so one hiccup would have converted the whole reopened backlog to "confirmed no trading happened" at 50 rows/min, unrecoverable (due query is `captured_at IS NULL`).  Empty now CORROBORATES via `backfill_attempts`, terminating only at `MAX_BACKFILL_ATTEMPTS` — which also removes the dependency on merging #2959 first.  Also: bar closes validated locally (a null close would write price=null+error=null and count as success), and the 11 FMP-402 rows reopened alongside the 2937.  Gates: `deno check` clean; 273 files / 3414 tests; lint 403 = baseline, zero added.  MUST RUN `POST /api/admin/migrate` AFTER MERGE — auto-deploy ships code but never schema.  Receipt: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-20-latency-snapshot-repair.md`.

- **2026-08-13 2:47pm CT — CLAUDE — COMPLETED/MERGED (#1835 `c38b6787`) + DEPLOYED (live sha `c38b67877745`; ToS now reads "14 days / 2 weeks" in prod) — iOS Premium: one screen, and an App Store purchase that actually confirms (branch `claude/ios-paywall-iap-latency`).** Owner bought Premium Monthly in TestFlight; Apple charged, the app spun, then showed red **"Request failed"** — and Restore Purchases later showed "Command is still running" while Premium had in fact been granted.  Root cause: `POST /api/client/v1/commands` only ENQUEUED `redeem_apple_purchase` on the durable queue, which nothing executes except the scheduled tick (live `paid` profile = every 60s, `free` = 5 min, and behind whatever ingest the tick claims first), while the iOS client polled for 40 attempts ~= 18.5s and then threw.  The redeem could not succeed inside the window the phone was willing to wait.  Fix: the POST now enqueues the durable backstop FIRST, then runs `executeQueuedCommand` inline under a 9s budget (kept under the client's 20s per-request timeout) and answers 200 with a terminal row instead of 202+queued; `executeQueuedCommand` already no-ops on a terminal row, so the later queue delivery is a cheap read, not a second execution.  Client side: the app had **no `Transaction.updates` observer anywhere** — a StoreKit 2 requirement — so Ask to Buy, renewals, interrupted purchases and retries of a failed redeem were all invisible; new `Store/AppleIAP.swift` adds the listener plus a quiet launch sweep, keeps `finish()` AFTER the server call so a failed redeem is redelivered rather than discarded, poll budget 18.5s -> ~77s, and purchase/restore failures now say Apple took the money and Restore is the fix instead of surfacing a raw HTTP string.  Also, per owner: the two Premium sheets collapse into one (`Views/Status/PremiumSheet.swift` replaces `SubscribeView.swift`; `PremiumInfoSheet` deleted from `Components.swift`; all six call sites repointed) — benefits + price + real StoreKit products + Restore on one screen, no "See Plans" hop.  The two sheets had already drifted (one said "2-week free trial", the other "1-month", same build); trial copy is now 2 weeks everywhere (`DeliveryView` x2, `FeedDashboardView`, `legalHtml.ts` "30 days / 1 month" -> "14 days / 2 weeks") behind one `PremiumPricing.headline`.  Gates: `npm run typecheck` clean; `npx vitest run src/client/__tests__/` 6 files / 73 tests green (11 route tests updated from the old always-202 contract, 1 new test pins the inline contract incl. backstop-drain-is-a-no-op); `xcodebuild ... BUILD SUCCEEDED`; paywall rendered on iPhone 17 Pro sim (signed-out state — StoreKit products need ASC sandbox, not verifiable in a bare simulator).  **OWNER FOLLOW-UP: confirm the App Store Connect introductory offer on `trade.congress.premium.monthly` / `.annual` is 2 weeks — copy now says 2 weeks and ASC is the authority.**  Rollout: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-13-ios-paywall-one-screen-and-inline-commands.md`.

- 2026-07-17 — MONET — In Progress — **Telemetry failsafe: circuit breaker + move usage-telemetry outbox off D1** (branch `monet/telemetry-failsafe-off-d1`, owner-directed, prevents recurrence of the usage.jays.services 2-day-OOM D1 overage incident, ~$1,153). Adds a durable CONFIG_KV circuit breaker (consecutive-failure threshold, exponential backoff 30s→30min capped, half-open probe on the next natural attempt) around `deliverUsageTelemetryEvent` in `app/src/shared/thirdPartyTelemetry.ts`; the queue consumer (`app/src/index.ts` `handleIngestMessage`/`handleDeadLetterMessage`) now routes new `usage.telemetry` events straight to the R2 outbox (no live delivery attempt, acks instead of retrying) while the circuit is open. Stops writing new rows to D1 `usage_telemetry_fallback_events` entirely — R2 is now the sole durable outbox for new events; a bounded one-time drain (read+delete, no re-churn UPDATE) empties pre-existing legacy D1 rows and a CONFIG_KV marker skips the D1 SELECT once observed empty. R2 outbox is now capped (`USAGE_TELEMETRY_FALLBACK_MAX_OBJECTS`, default 5000, drops new writes at cap) and TTL-expired (`USAGE_TELEMETRY_FALLBACK_TTL_DAYS`, default 14d, checked during flush). `flushUsageTelemetryFallback` skips listing R2/querying D1 entirely while the circuit is open. All limits env-overridable (see `app/src/shared/types.ts`). Added/updated tests in `thirdPartyTelemetry.test.ts`, `thirdPartyTelemetryQueue.test.ts`, `admin/__tests__/batchStatusUsage.test.ts` (updated 4 tests that relied on the old D1-fallback-of-last-resort behavior to instead exercise the R2 fallback). Gates green: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, full `npm test` 132 files / 1392 tests pass. Migration/table left in place per instructions (no drop in this PR — future cleanup). Committed locally, NOT pushed/PR'd/deployed — owner gates landing per task brief. #agent-sync claim posted (KEEPOUT on thirdPartyTelemetry.ts + index.ts usage.telemetry routing until this lands).

- **2026-08-13 3:37pm CT — MONET — IN PROGRESS — CI and iOS ship never ran on bot-merged PRs (branch `monet/ci-ship-trigger-bot-merge`).**  A PR merged by `github-actions[bot]` lands on `main` and dispatches ZERO workflow runs (GitHub raises no workflow events for `GITHUB_TOKEN` actions; `auto-merge-prs.yml` arms with that token).  Verified: `c38b6787` (#1835, bot) -> 0 runs; `ceaca097` (#1836, human) -> 10, five `event: push`.  Three layers: auto-merge workflows refuse to arm without `GH_PAT`/`SHEPHERD_TOKEN` (absent in all three fleet repos) and print the manual `gh pr merge --squash --auto` instead; `ci.yml` gains an hourly `schedule:` backstop behind a fail-closed gate; `ios-ship.yml` gains `cron: '7,37 * * * *'` + a tested `scripts/ios-fleet/scheduled-ship-gate.sh` so a cron cannot ship TestFlight builds for backend-only commits.  Drift step made advisory (`check-drift.sh` exits 1 today and is not repairable from inside the repo).  OWNER: add `GH_PAT`; reinstall `/Users/jay/apps/ios-fleet` from the repo.  **Review round 2 (before landing):** (a) the required `typecheck + test` job used `needs: [schedule-gate]` + `if: should_run == '1'`, so a FAILED or timed-out gate job would mark it `skipped` -- which GitHub reports as a SATISFIED required check, i.e. a PR could merge with tsc/tests never run; all three gated jobs now use `!cancelled() && (event != 'schedule' || should_run != '0')` (the pattern ST adopted in its PR #370).  (b) this repo ships from the IN-REPO `scripts/ios-fleet/` copy, which still carried the wrong-build export-compliance defect verbatim (`ensure-tf-ready` with no build number -> `sort=-uploadedDate&limit=1` -> the PREVIOUS ship, because ASC ingestion is async), so enabling the cron would have made every automatic CT ship silently uninstallable; both files were byte-identical to the pre-edit runtime backup, so the fixed runtime `asc-api.mjs` + `ship-testflight.sh` were copied in whole (`test-ship-seq.sh` still 43/43).  Rollout: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-13-ci-ship-trigger-bot-merge.md`.

- **[Congress.Trade][CLAUDE] Delivery/client input hardening, command lifecycle, honest tx_type, detail privacy+budget (2026-07-19) — PR OPENED.** Branch `claude/delivery-client-v3`. Scope confined to `app/src/delivery/`, `app/src/client/`, `app/docs/client-mobile-api.md` per assignment; verified against current main first (PR #592's SSE→WebSocket-DO work touched only `clients/pwa/`, not `app/src/delivery`; PR #620's resource-governors work touches `deadLetter.ts`/`outbox.ts`/`webhook.ts`/`targetCircuit.ts`, disjoint from this PR's files — no overlap either way). Seven items: (1) fractional/invalid `?limit=`/`?offset=`/export `maxRows` now floored before the `<=0`/cap clamp in `buildTransactionsQuery`/`buildTransactionsExportQuery` (`rows.ts`) — was already mid-flight in the worktree's one dirty file, finished + tested (NaN/Infinity/fractional cases). (2) Zero-delta `?since=` polls (both `/api/transactions` and `/api/client/v1/feed`) now skip the full `COUNT(*)` + today-filings aggregate entirely, omitting `total`/`filingsImportedToday` rather than lying with `0` — verified both known clients (dashboard `fetchUpdates()`, PWA `poll()`) already gate every read of those fields behind "got new rows back?" and no-op otherwise, so this is a pure cost cut with no client-visible behavior change. (3) `POST /api/client/v1/commands` idempotency is now race-safe: a true concurrent-duplicate INSERT collision (`DuplicateCommandError` on the unique `(user_id, idempotency_key)` index) replays the winner instead of 500ing, and a `queued`/`running` row whose owning request died mid-flight is reclaimed + re-run after `STALE_IN_FLIGHT_COMMAND_TTL_MS` (2 min) instead of being replayed forever. (4) `tx_type` NULL passthrough (also mid-flight in the dirty file, finished): `mapTransaction` no longer defaults a malformed/undisclosed side to `'P'`; confirmed downstream aggregates (`tickerSummarySql`/`memberSummarySql`), `matchesFilters`' `sides` filter, and `csvCell` all already handle a non-matching/null `tx_type` correctly, and the legacy dashboard's `txToRow()` already has its own `tx.txType || 'P'` display fallback — no client regression. (5) `GET /filings/:docId` (public, previously unauthenticated AND unbudgeted) now applies the same per-IP daily row budget as the feed and returns `toPublicFiling()` instead of the raw internal `Filing` (was leaking `rawObjectKey` R2 key, `extractor`/`modelVersion` model slugs, and raw `error` text to anonymous callers); `/api/client/v1/{trade/:id,ticker/:ticker,member/:memberIdOrName}` also gained the row-budget check `/feed` already had. (6) `resolveMember` (client) and the feed's `memberName` filter (delivery, shared code path) now escape `%`/`_`/`\` before building the `LIKE` pattern (new `escapeLikePattern` in `rows.ts`, paired with `ESCAPE ''` in the SQL) so a literal wildcard character in a search term is matched literally, not as a SQL wildcard. (7) "Premium CSV export accepts bearer, not just cookie" — verified against main: PR #558 ("remove all column gating and CSV export premium limits") deleted the entire premium gate on `/api/export/transactions.csv`, including the cookie-only `getCurrentUser()` check the item describes — the endpoint is now fully public with NO auth check of any kind, so the described bug no longer exists to fix (confirmed via `git show 02b78cf`, which shows the deleted gate literally used `getCurrentUser`, not `getCurrentUserFromRequest`). No code change for #7 beyond a comment correcting stale "premium" language; flagged in the PR body in case reinstating a premium-gated export is wanted as an explicit, separate product decision (must use `getCurrentUserFromRequest` this time). Added ~30 new tests across `rows.ts`/`queries.ts`/`routes.ts`. Gates: `npm run typecheck` clean; `npm test` 1697/1697 own-scope tests green across 3 full-suite runs — the only flakes observed were 1-4 timeouts in `src/admin/__tests__/reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` (untouched by this PR), which passes 19/19 in isolation every time (confirmed twice), i.e. pre-existing machine-load flakiness, not a regression.
- 2026-07-19 — CLAUDE — In Progress (PR #653, squash auto-merge armed) — **Agreement-fingerprint text normalization** (branch `claude/agreement-fingerprint-normalization`). Production evidence: the agreement cascade was reading documents successfully but had 0 publishes in 6+ hours (324/324 candidates `cascade_unresolved`). Root cause: `materialRowFingerprint`/`sameRowSet`/`buildMajorityRows` in `app/src/extraction/agreement.ts` compared every row field — including free text like `assetName`/`description` — through byte-strict `canonicalText` (trim+uppercase+whitespace-collapse only), so cross-vendor formatting drift ("First Data Corp." vs "FIRST DATA CORP") blocked agreement on filings every model actually read correctly. Fix: the 9 strict/enum fields (ticker, amounts, tx_type, tx_date, owner, is_option, cap_gains_over_200, filing_status) keep byte/value equality always; the 7 free-text fields now compare via a new `canonicalAgreementText` — casefold + punctuation-strip + the EXISTING `cleanAssetString` company-suffix canonicalizer (no new normalizer invented) — one comparator shared across tier-1/2 unanimity and tier-3 majority voting, with near-miss agreement preferring the higher-confidence contributing model's own text. Kill switch `AGREEMENT_TEXT_NORMALIZATION` (default true). Picked up substantial prior work already in the worktree from an earlier interrupted attempt (reviewed, sound, matched spec); found and fixed 2 wiring gaps left from that attempt (admin `/config-sources` diagnostics registry + `.dev.vars.example` both missing the new var vs every sibling `AGREEMENT_*` tunable) and one deterministic test-data bug (a tier-3 test used `ticker: null`, which routes row-grouping through `arbitrationRowKey`'s pre-existing, untouched, out-of-scope raw-text fallback instead of the new comparator — fixed by giving the test rows a ticker). Gates: typecheck clean; all 8 agreement-related test files green (73/73); full suite 1677/1685 — the 8 failures are `reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` timeouts, verified pre-existing/unrelated by reproducing identically against clean `main` with this diff fully reverted (fleet-wide host-load contention, not a regression).

  2026-07-12.** Every filing fetch in production failed from 2026-07-11T19:14Z onward with
  `fetcher: Provided readable stream must have a known length` — PR #284's `limitedFilingBody`

  length; upstream Content-Length doesn't carry over, and OGE's Domino server sends chunked with
  no Content-Length at all). Casualties: all 500 filings of a just-started H-2015 house backfill
  (ingestion_outbox rows dead-lettered to `failed` after the 5-cycle cap) + all 17 executive OGE
  278-Ts discovered by the first post-#315 watcher poll (still cycling, self-recover on fix).
  The fetcher unit tests mocked `RAW_FILES.put` as stream-draining, so CI could not catch it.
  Fix: `bufferFilingBody()` buffers through the existing byte-count guard (25MB cap intact) and
  hands R2 a known-length `Uint8Array`; regression test pins a no-Content-Length chunked response
  (put receives buffered bytes, never a bare stream). Recovery: new
  `POST /api/admin/ingest-requeue-failed` (`requeueFailedIngestionOutbox`: failed→pending with
  fresh dead-letter budget, optional docIdPrefix, dryRun) + the per-minute outbox flush drains the
  backlog. Gates: typecheck + 111 files / 977 tests green. Playbook: merge → `deploy.yml` →
  requeue failed rows → verify fetches resume (receipts in #agent-sync closeout).

- **[Congress.Trade][CLAUDE] Delivery/client input hardening, command lifecycle, honest tx_type, detail privacy+budget (2026-07-19) — PR OPENED.** Branch `claude/delivery-client-v3`. Scope confined to `app/src/delivery/`, `app/src/client/`, `app/docs/client-mobile-api.md` per assignment; verified against current main first (PR #592's SSE→WebSocket-DO work touched only `clients/pwa/`, not `app/src/delivery`; PR #620's resource-governors work touches `deadLetter.ts`/`outbox.ts`/`webhook.ts`/`targetCircuit.ts`, disjoint from this PR's files — no overlap either way). Seven items: (1) fractional/invalid `?limit=`/`?offset=`/export `maxRows` now floored before the `<=0`/cap clamp in `buildTransactionsQuery`/`buildTransactionsExportQuery` (`rows.ts`) — was already mid-flight in the worktree's one dirty file, finished + tested (NaN/Infinity/fractional cases). (2) Zero-delta `?since=` polls (both `/api/transactions` and `/api/client/v1/feed`) now skip the full `COUNT(*)` + today-filings aggregate entirely, omitting `total`/`filingsImportedToday` rather than lying with `0` — verified both known clients (dashboard `fetchUpdates()`, PWA `poll()`) already gate every read of those fields behind "got new rows back?" and no-op otherwise, so this is a pure cost cut with no client-visible behavior change. (3) `POST /api/client/v1/commands` idempotency is now race-safe: a true concurrent-duplicate INSERT collision (`DuplicateCommandError` on the unique `(user_id, idempotency_key)` index) replays the winner instead of 500ing, and a `queued`/`running` row whose owning request died mid-flight is reclaimed + re-run after `STALE_IN_FLIGHT_COMMAND_TTL_MS` (2 min) instead of being replayed forever. (4) `tx_type` NULL passthrough (also mid-flight in the dirty file, finished): `mapTransaction` no longer defaults a malformed/undisclosed side to `'P'`; confirmed downstream aggregates (`tickerSummarySql`/`memberSummarySql`), `matchesFilters`' `sides` filter, and `csvCell` all already handle a non-matching/null `tx_type` correctly, and the legacy dashboard's `txToRow()` already has its own `tx.txType || 'P'` display fallback — no client regression. (5) `GET /filings/:docId` (public, previously unauthenticated AND unbudgeted) now applies the same per-IP daily row budget as the feed and returns `toPublicFiling()` instead of the raw internal `Filing` (was leaking `rawObjectKey` R2 key, `extractor`/`modelVersion` model slugs, and raw `error` text to anonymous callers); `/api/client/v1/{trade/:id,ticker/:ticker,member/:memberIdOrName}` also gained the row-budget check `/feed` already had. (6) `resolveMember` (client) and the feed's `memberName` filter (delivery, shared code path) now escape `%`/`_`/`\` before building the `LIKE` pattern (new `escapeLikePattern` in `rows.ts`, paired with `ESCAPE ''` in the SQL) so a literal wildcard character in a search term is matched literally, not as a SQL wildcard. (7) "Premium CSV export accepts bearer, not just cookie" — verified against main: PR #558 ("remove all column gating and CSV export premium limits") deleted the entire premium gate on `/api/export/transactions.csv`, including the cookie-only `getCurrentUser()` check the item describes — the endpoint is now fully public with NO auth check of any kind, so the described bug no longer exists to fix (confirmed via `git show 02b78cf`, which shows the deleted gate literally used `getCurrentUser`, not `getCurrentUserFromRequest`). No code change for #7 beyond a comment correcting stale "premium" language; flagged in the PR body in case reinstating a premium-gated export is wanted as an explicit, separate product decision (must use `getCurrentUserFromRequest` this time). Added ~30 new tests across `rows.ts`/`queries.ts`/`routes.ts`. Gates: `npm run typecheck` clean; `npm test` 1697/1697 own-scope tests green across 3 full-suite runs — the only flakes observed were 1-4 timeouts in `src/admin/__tests__/reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` (untouched by this PR), which passes 19/19 in isolation every time (confirmed twice), i.e. pre-existing machine-load flakiness, not a regression.

Root cause: `materialRowFingerprint`/`sameRowSet`/`buildMajorityRows` in `app/src/extraction/agreement.ts` compared every row field — including free text like `assetName`/`description` — through byte-strict `canonicalText` (trim+uppercase+whitespace-collapse only), so cross-vendor formatting drift ("First Data Corp." vs "FIRST DATA CORP") blocked agreement on filings every model actually read correctly. Kill switch `AGREEMENT_TEXT_NORMALIZATION` (default true). Picked up substantial prior work already in the worktree from an earlier interrupted attempt (reviewed, sound, matched spec); found and fixed 2 wiring gaps left from that attempt (admin `/config-sources` diagnostics registry + `.dev.vars.example` both missing the new var vs every sibling `AGREEMENT_*` tunable) and one deterministic test-data bug (a tier-3 test used `ticker: null`, which routes row-grouping through `arbitrationRowKey`'s pre-existing, untouched, out-of-scope raw-text fallback instead of the new comparator — fixed by giving the test rows a ticker). New env knobs (`app/src/shared/types.ts` + `app/docs/config-registry.md`): `USAGE_MONITOR_READ_TOKEN`, `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_THROTTLE_ENABLED` (default on), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_THROTTLE_THRESHOLD` (default 1.0 = monitor's "exceeded"), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_STATUS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (default 120000), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 5000, capped 15000). Composes strictly UNDER PR #620's hard LLM-USD governor once that lands; does not touch `bakeoff.ts`/`configuredVision.ts`/`openRouterVision.ts`. **FAILS OPEN on any error, timeout, non-2xx, malformed body, or missing config — never throttles when it can't get a clean answer** (explicitly tested: unreachable, timeout, 401, malformed JSON, unconfigured provider, disabled switch). New env knobs (`app/src/shared/types.ts` + `app/docs/config-registry.md`): `USAGE_MONITOR_READ_TOKEN`, `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_THROTTLE_ENABLED` (default on), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_THROTTLE_THRESHOLD` (default 1.0 = monitor's "exceeded"), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_STATUS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (default 120000), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 5000, capped 15000). Gates: 17/17 new `monitorBudgetGate.test.ts` cases green, 18/18 existing `autopilot.test.ts` green, `npm run typecheck` clean (after `npm ci` refreshed a stale `node_modules` — installed `@jaywedgeworth22/congress-trading-shared` was v1.8.3 vs the v1.10.0 commit pinned in package.json, the exact stale-install symptom flagged fleet-wide in #agent-sync today; unrelated to this change). No KEEPOUT collision found in #agent-sync history on `monitorBudgetGate.ts`/`autopilot.ts`/`shared/types.ts`. Not merging — leaving PR green/ready for owner review per branch-protection.

- **[Congress.Trade][CLAUDE] Enrichment/prices reliability bundle — PR #652 OPENED, auto-merge armed (2026-07-19).** Picked up and finished a nearly-complete branch (`claude/enrichment-prices-v3`). Seven items: (1) enrichment transient-failure retry — a thrown provider error now writes an attempt-aged `transient-retry:<n>:<iso>` backoff marker (1h→24h doubling cap) instead of tombstoning; only a clean all-providers "no data" result still tombstones. (2) SEC EDGAR ticker-map fetch failures are no longer cached as a permanent empty `Map` (was silently blinding every subsequent lookup for the isolate's life). (3) price refresh aborts the rest of the run on an auth/plan/rate-limit error (401/402/403/429) instead of burning the remaining daily budget on daily calls guaranteed to fail identically. (4) every attempted FMP call (success or throw) now counts against the shared daily meter, matching real billing. (5) `spx_at_trade`/`spx_at_filing` now `COALESCE` onto the prior value on conflict so an incomplete same-run `spx_eod` fetch never regresses a good anchor to null. (6) `selectTickersNeedingPrices` now orders oldest-traded-first (`cursor_seq ASC`) so new trades can't perpetually crowd out an unpriced backlog. (7) not-found backoff is now two-stage (7d first miss, 30d second+/stalled) — fixed a downstream consistency bug this uncovered in `admin/routes.ts`s `marketPending`, which only excluded `price_unavailable=1` under one cutoff and would have over-reported "pending" for stage-2/stalled tickers. Also added: `jobs.ts` now reserves a 20%-of-cap price-refresh budget floor (gated on an FMP key being configured) so enrichment (which runs first on the shared daily FMP counter) can't starve price refresh to zero. No new migration (`price_unavailable` already a plain unconstrained INTEGER). Gate: typecheck clean; full suite 150/151 files, 1693/1698 tests green — the only 5 failures are pre-existing `Miniflare`/`workerd` timeouts in `reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` (documented elsewhere in this log as fragile under resource-constrained hosts; zero references to any file this PR touches, reproduced in isolation under this host's very high concurrent multi-agent load). Added/completed test coverage for all seven items.

Modified `.github/workflows/deploy-deno.yml` to trigger deployments on a 3-hour cron schedule (`0 */3 * * *`) rather than on every `push`. - **2026-07-27** - `[Congress.Trade]`: Deployment Deduplication Fix (AG) — COMPLETED. Modified `.github/workflows/deploy-deno.yml`'s python pre-check script to interrogate the GitHub Actions API for the most recent successful run of the deploy workflow. If the `head_sha` of the last successful run identically matches the current `GITHUB_SHA` being triggered by the cron, the workflow exits early and skips deploying to Deno Deploy. This robustly ensures compute time is never burned if there are no code updates.

(4) host.jays.services (Coolify UI, also 502) pointed at tailscale IP `100.97.154.2:8000` unreachable from inside the container → now `reverse_proxy coolify:8080` over the shared `coolify` network. Also confirmed P0-4 live by executing `fmtCompany` from the deployed bundle (`MICROSOFT CORP` → `Microsoft Corp.`). **FLEET NOTE: Coolify API works at `https://host.jays.services` with `COOLIFY_AGENTS` ONLY with a browser User-Agent — Cloudflare 403s every other agent on every path and it looks exactly like an auth failure.** PR #1238 (adversarial review of AG's merged work, 15 agents, findings verified then re-refuted): (1) **deploy revision receipt** — `/api/health` reports `build.sha`, `ship.sh` asserts it BEFORE migrating and fails on mismatch, ending this class of false "deployed"; (2) **migrate would have failed the next deploy** — the list rebuilds a superseded strict UNIQUE index that a later statement drops, throwing `UNIQUE constraint failed` when a deprecated row shares `(doc_id,source,row_key)` with a live one (harmless while migrate swallowed errors, fatal now #1230 fails closed); (3) **`tx_cursor_seq` unindexed on `tx_id`** — both #1232 repair statements AND the trigger itself (runs on EVERY INSERT) correlate on it → full scan of one-row-per-transaction-ever (~76k+); repair was O(poisoned × 76k) in an HTTP request and ingestion degrades quadratically; (4) **cursor trigger swap silently drops trades** — `DROP`+`CREATE` on the same name with no enclosing transaction leaves a window with NO trigger; an INSERT there gets `cursor_seq = NULL`, invisible to EVERY feed read (`cursor_seq > ?` applies even at `since=0`) and unhealable by a repair keyed on `>= 1e12` (NULL >= 1e12 is NULL); reopened every deploy. Fixed via create-`_v2`-then-drop-v1 + NULL-healing repair + readiness accepting either name (else health 503s between deploy and migrate); verified on real SQLite, idempotent across two runs; (5) readiness postcondition **failed open** when the DB was unreachable — the exact case it exists to catch; (6) `ship.sh` used `curl -fsS`, discarding migrate's entire `failed[]` diagnostic on 500; (7) 3 tracked files still carried `SQLITE_WEB_PASSWORD:-admin` (applying them resurrects it) — removed + `*.orig`/`*.rej` gitignored; (8) the 25 rewritten `.py` scripts failed SILENTLY on a missing password (empty password → login HTML returned as data) — now raise; (9) new **CI guard** blocking fail-open secret defaults, password literals, and unauthenticated `sqlite_web` — verified it catches all three originals at `173a3ba8`; (10) shared-package pin check could **pass by doing nothing** (`|| true` swallowed git's error exit); (11) **P1-15** dead GA tag removed — prod injected `G-B3J0XHK0FX` while CSP is `script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'`, so it never loaded once; guard test asserts every external `<script src>` origin is actually allowed by the CSP the same module emits. Every guard mutation-tested. Gates: `deno check` clean, **2025/2025**, merged origin/main (#1237/#1239). STILL OPEN/UNCLAIMED from the audit: P0-2 health-depth, P1-2 KV prefix leak (session tokens in the browsable SQLite DB; fix logs all users out — needs owner timing), P1-8 CSV entitlement (product decision), P1-16 webhook replay window, P1-17 queue retention, P1-18 migration drift, P2 UI/a11y + docs drift, iOS P0-6/7/8.

- **2026-08-20 — CLAUDE — COMPLETED / PR OPEN — Remove forced registration from IAP purchase (Guideline 5.1.1(v), submission b61e2a4a).** Apple rejected b61e2a4a for requiring account registration before an In-App Purchase (PDF/CSV) that is not itself account-based. Hybrid entitlement model: `apple_subscriptions.user_id` made nullable (migration 0090, table rebuild — SQLite can't drop NOT NULL) so a signed-out device can redeem via a new unauthenticated `POST /api/client/v1/entitlements/apple/redeem` (`billing/appleRedeem.ts` shares the exact JWS-chain/bundle/sandbox/product verification with the authenticated path — no second copy of the money logic). Anonymous redeem writes `user_id: NULL` and mints a short-lived (`min(expiry,24h)`) HMAC-signed device entitlement token (`billing/deviceEntitlement.ts`, new `APPLE_DEVICE_ENTITLEMENT_SECRET`); the device presents it as `X-Apple-Device-Entitlement` on the two content-only routes (`GET /api/documents/:docId/pdf`, `GET /api/export/transactions.csv`), which re-check the LIVE ledger row every request (a session always wins, never OR'd with a device token). New authenticated `link_apple_entitlement` command claims a null-owner row on sign-in, silently (409 = already linked elsewhere, not surfaced except on explicit Restore Purchases). `upsertAppleSubscription`'s owner-mismatch guard now lets a null owner through but still refuses reassigning an OWNED row — caught and fixed a real bug in the process: the UPSERT's `DO UPDATE SET` never reassigned `user_id`, so a "successful" claim silently stayed null-owned (test-driven, caught before merge). Delivery/webhook/push stay sign-in-gated (Apple's own guideline exemption for account-tied functionality) — untouched. iOS: `PremiumSheet` no longer blocks purchase on sign-in — shows the same plan buttons signed in or out, an anonymous-subscribed state, and an optional "Sign in — it's optional" link (Apple's own suggested framing) that never interrupts a purchase; `AppleIAP.swift` branches redeem on `signedIn`; `APIClient` attaches the cached device token to PDF/CSV requests; a second `KeychainTokenStore(service:)` instance holds it separately from the session token. Gates: `npm run typecheck` + `npm test` green (271 files / 3369 tests, all new: anonymous-redeem-accepted, forged/sandbox-JWS-rejected, already-linked-cannot-be-claimed-by-a-second-account, revoked-device-token-refused, session-always-wins-over-device-token). iOS: `xcodebuild … build` succeeded; `xcodebuild … test` ran the full 300-case suite — all 7 new/touched Apple-purchase tests passed; 6 unrelated pre-existing tests failed with clear environmental signatures (`XCTUnwrap(request.httpBody)` nil — a known OS httpBody/httpBodyStream timing quirk this same file already works around elsewhere; `XCTUnwrap(MockURLProtocol.handler)` nil on a test that makes no network call at all) under severe host contention (a single `xcodebuild test` took ~24 min; a plain `simctl install` needed multiple retries) — none touch Apple purchase/entitlement code. Fixed one unrelated pre-existing test-target compile bug (`try XCTUnwrap(try? …)` in a non-`throws` test method) that was blocking `xcodebuild test` entirely before this PR. Live simulator screenshot NOT captured — CoreSimulator was too contended today (install/launch repeatedly failed/hung); code-level walkthrough substitutes, screenshot to follow when the host is calmer.
